    <encodingDesc>
      <projectDesc>
        <p>Gpura corpus: a structured, machine-readable companion to Gpura's scans. The PAGE-XML and ALTO files for the same pages share this file's region and line identifiers.</p>
      </projectDesc>
      <samplingDecl>
        <p>Line boxes were detected automatically from the page images. Each line's reading is Gpura's existing OCR text for that line, aligned to the detected line by fuzzy matching against a Tesseract reading. Nothing has been checked by a person. Illustrations, maps and reader handwriting are not encoded.</p>
      </samplingDecl>
      <editorialDecl>
        <normalization>
          <p>Diplomatic: wording and spelling as in Gpura's text, which follows the print. Character encoding only: atomic chillus; NTA as ന്റ and TTA as റ്റ however the source spelled them; NFC. Where this changed Gpura's string, PAGE-XML keeps the original.</p>
        </normalization>
        <hyphenation>
          <p>The print breaks words at line end without a hyphen. &lt;lb break='no'/&gt; marks a line that continues the previous line's word. In automatic encodings this is an estimate from the book's own vocabulary and Malayalam script rules.</p>
        </hyphenation>
        <interpretation>
          <p>&lt;unclear&gt; marks a line whose reading is not Gpura's text (cert='low': Tesseract only; reason='ai-reading', cert='medium': read by an AI model from the line image) or whose match to the detected line is weak (cert='medium').</p>
        </interpretation>
      </editorialDecl>
    </encodingDesc>
